- **ddd-domain-event**: Add a domain event to an aggregate in a bounded context (Core/&lt;Context&gt;/). Define the event as a record implementing IDomainEvent (in &lt;Context&gt;.Domain/&lt;Aggregates&gt;/Events), raise it from a static Create factory or a named mutator on the aggregate via AddDomainEvent, and write an INotificationHandler&lt;TEvent&gt; in &lt;Context&gt;.Application/&lt;Aggregates&gt;/EventHandlers. Events dispatch in-process through MediatR inside EfCoreUnitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync before the DB save. Use when an aggregate state change needs an in-context reaction (or to bridge to an integration event — see ddd-integration-event). ([Pieter-1337/DDD](https://github.com/Pieter-1337/DDD/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ddd-domain-event))
